Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in nanotechnology theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 299 and the invariant I(A,B) = 372 mod 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to nanotechnology theory, we decompose the problem into 299 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 372. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
